I de Gener. et corrupt. text.23 anchor + virtus-daemonis-non-spiritualior-quam-substantia / 4 templum Dei per gratiam = templum diaboli per peccatum + ubi-erat-gratia-incipit-esse-culpa) + 4 *Contra* (1 Aug. *de Fide ad Petrum* c.3 n.30 *Inest singulis spiritibus naturalis terminus quo a se invicem distinguuntur et unus in altero non est* / 2 unus-illabitur-aut-alter-aut-non dilemma: si-omnes-spiritus-mali-uni-animae plane falsum, si-unus-implet-totam-animam imaginem-Dei-nihil-minus-Deo-implere-potest Aug.+Beda XII de Civ. **Session 56 (2026-05-20) — `d8-p1-a3-q2` Tier-2 (d.8 8/16; ART III Q.II — CLOSED).** *Utrum Angeli in corporibus assumtis exerceant operationes convenientes potentiae sensitivae.* Raw 15902–16057, **pp.221–223** (PDF 243–245), ~2042 w Latin — ties with s54 q2 + s55 q1 as the HEAVIEST d.8 chunks. Per s55's SCHOLION III prediction this q.2 carries **NO own scholion**; the chunk's terminal apparatus entry (`[^18]: Vide scholion ad praecedentem quaest.`) is Quaracchi's own cross-pointer to q.1's SCHOLION III–IV block, locking the Vol II shared-question-scholion convention. Structure: 7 *videtur* (1 *idem est principium motus et sensus* Arist. *De Somno* c.3 / 2 organa apta ad motum ↔ organa apta ad sensum / 3 operatio consequens proprium speciei + proprium generis — risibile/sensibile / 4 sensus cognitivus particularium / 5 *immateriali* vs *alligari corpori* + the famous **Angelus Romae audit nos loquentes Parisius** distance thought-experiment / 6 *crematur ab igne* a-fortiori / 7 *suscipit in oculo similitudinem* + **reception + judgment** as definition of sense; textual variant fn. 4 = Vat. moves *et* clause-position altering causal-proposition force) + **Ad oppositum / Fundamenta** 6 (1 sensus praesupponit vitam; back-ref q.1 + a.2 q.1 / 2 sensus praesupponit actum vegetativae / 3 si sentiret per corpus → natus uniri corpori / 4 actio coniuncti → ergo communicaret poenam et gloriam reductio / 5 sentire proprium animalis / 6 mediante organo corporeo → non cognosceret clauso oculo / a tergo / in obscuro — grounds the *receptio + iudicium* analysis in Solutio 7) + *Conclusio* (*Angelus in corpore assumto opera potentiae sensitivae non exercet, quatenus sunt actus sentiendi per organa, sed quatenus sunt ab anima ut movente*) + **Respondeo** with the **triple-mode operation-analysis** (rhetorical/doctrinal heart of d.8 P.I, lynchpin for Christology + later eschatology): *Triplex est genus operationum* (anima ut **motor** → *per* corpus + *in* corpus / anima ut **perfectio** → *cum* corpore *anima clauso oculo corpus aliquod videre non potest* / **tertiae** *quasi utroque modo*) keyed to *hoc aliquid + motor differens a mobili* (= *substantia prima* per p.222 n.1 cross-ref to p.197 nota 3) vs *forma iuncta materiae* — fn.7 is textual crux (Vat. + cod. cc + ed. 1 carry *anima clauso oculo* clause older mss. lack; Quaracchi adopts longer + glosses *hoc aliquid* = *substantia prima*); descent-by-example: *motus progressivus* (motor → YES Angelo) / *sentire per organa* (perfectio → NO) / *ridere* (both — *cordis exhilaratio* perfectio + *genarum motio* motor → only motor-part competit); *Conclusio bimembris* (sensitive *per organa* NO; sensitive *ab anima ut movente* YES — communicat ea corpori) + **Solutio oppositorum** 7 (1 Philosophus de animalibus / 2 ***signum* vs *instrumentum*** — corpus assumitur **ad ostensionem sui ipsius** non ad sensificationem organorum (showability/instrumentality distinction anchors angelophany doctrine) / 3 *ridere* gloss: motus genarum (motor) vs actus interior (perfectio) Arist. *III de Part. animal.* c.10 / 4 **locked *Sentire dicitur tripliciter* division** — *communiter* (rem ut praesentem cognoscere — Aug. *de Videndo Deo ad Paulinam* = Ep. 147 c.2 n.7) / *proprie* (*hic et nunc* — Boethius *Comm. in Porphyr.* + *de Consol.* V prosa 4 *sensus particularium intellectus universalium*) / *magis proprie* (species in materia praeter materiam per organum — Philos. *II de Anima* text. 121 c.12); administrativa descendit ad nos cum solatio); triple *indigent solatio assumti corporis* = *ad operationes exercendas / ad se ipsos manifestandos / ad nosmetipsos laetificandos sive confortandos*; triple analogy *assumunt sicut instrumenta vel organa ad operandum / sicut signa ad manifestandum / sicut cooperimenta vel habitacula ad conversandum* with the *coniungitur instrumentum motori / signum significatori / habitaculum inhabitatori* tripled-correspondence; *finis imponit necessitatem his quae sunt ad finem* + *humana effigie insignitum* aesthetic argument that the body assumed must be specifically human-shaped (*tanquam pulchrum indumentum quodam modo decorat*) + **Ad rationes ad oppositum** 5 grouped replies (anima exuta *non habet vim administrativam* / *proportio sicut motoris ad mobile* not *perfectionis ad perfectibile* / *competit ad ministerium non ad esse completum* + *nec carentia indigentiam nec assumtio indecentiam* / *non perficit per corpus tanquam anima sed tanquam artifex per instrumentum* + *sicut instrumentum per quod quis meretur non remuneratur* / *non sufficienter dividit* + *homo assumit vestimentum sine unione in persona vel natura* counter-example + the *Filius Dei assumsit humanam naturam* perfect-union limit) + *Ex dictis* epilogue clarifying *Angelus se habet in ratione assumentis et habentis, corpus vero in ratione assumti et habiti* + editorial **SCHOLION I–II** on p.213 (I: triple-mode taxonomy of *assumtio* — *informatio* (substantial form, human soul) / *hypostatica unio* (Filius Dei) / *accidentalis unio* (instrumentum, signum, habitaculum) — + Uldaricus-error rejection [Angelos nunquam assumere corpora, apparitiones per visiones imaginarias = S. Thomas vocat erroneam] + *de Angelis bonis principaliter* gloss + forward cross-ref to a.2 q.1 on *Angelus non potest esse forma substantialis*; II: cross-ref roll = Alex. Hal. S. p.II q.34 m.2.3.4 / Scotus / S. Thom. + S. I q.51 a.2 + de Potent. q.6 a.7 / B. Albert. + S. p.I tr.18 q.75 m.1 / Petr. a Tar. q. unica a.2 quaestiunc.1 / Richard. a Med. / Aegid. R.). Dei ex Apuleii «Daemones sunt genere animalia, mente rationalia, animo passiva, corpore aerea, duratione aeterna» / Gregorius Hom. 10. in Evang. de Epiphania «rationale animal id est Angelus» / Bernardus Cant. hom. tertia «omnem spiritum creatum corporeo prorsus indigere solatio» / *ratio* on *nobilior-est-multiplicior-in-potentiis* with the *anima vivificat corpus* a fortiori / *ratio* on *influens-vitam-nobilius-est* with the *signum perfectionis* gloss) + **Ad oppositum** in 5 (Damascenus *Angelus est substantia intellectualis incorporea* / Dionysius *divinae mentes et spiritualia lumina immateriales et incorporei* / *nobilior-est-Deo-similior* with the *universum acephalum* Richard-of-St-Victor gloss [Ex Graeco ἀκέφαλον i. e. sine capite + the long IV *de Trin.* c. 25 *universitatis fabrica sine optimo genere creaturae* quotation] / *aliquid componitur ex diversis* + Aristot. VIII *Phys.* text 37 *movens-non-motum* corollary / *homo-peccavit-mortalis* with the *Angeli peccaverunt mortales* a-pari) + *Conclusio* blockquote (*Angeli boni et mali non habent corpora nec naturaliter sibi unita, nec inseparabiliter alligata*) + multi-modal **Respondeo** (Augustinus + Bernardus *dubie loquuntur* / Richardus *affirmat* Angelos esse naturaliter incorporeos / the est de lege communi* qualification / cross-ref to Sentences IV d.44 p.II a.3 q.2 for *spiritus absque corpore secundum ordinem divinae iustitiae ab igne materiali puniri* — i.e. the standard locus that demons' fiery torment doesn't entail inseparably-bound bodies) + **Ad argumenta** 5 grouped replies (Augustinus = *secundum opinionem Apuleii non sententiam suam* / Gregorius = doubled non propter se*, with the second Bernardus quote *Angelici spiritus ad ea capienda quae bonam faciunt creaturam nullo modo suis corporibus adiuvantur* — *non habent corpora simpliciter sed ad tempus* > ⚠ **Vol II rechunk-script bug (fixed session 13, do NOT regress):** the Vol II raw has **1056 form feeds (`\f`)**. Python `str.splitlines()` splits on `\f` too → every line number drifts ~1055 off from `grep`/`awk` (which all boundary numbers are derived with). `rechunk_d2.py` uses `RAW.read_text().split("\n")`. **Every future Vol II rechunk script must do the same** (d.3+). The first run of `rechunk_d2.py` used `splitlines()` and silently produced chunks starting mid-d.1-dubia — caught only by eyes-on PDF cross-check.

### Apparatus / PDF discipline (carry forward — proven sessions 6–7)

Session 7 (q2, ~2160 words, pp. 41–43) confirmed: for any large quaestio the IA OCR cascade-fragments the Respondeo and *all* page-footers. The reliable workflow: `python3.11 tools/extract-pages.py --volume vol2 --pages <printed> --dpi 450`, then a PIL helper that crops each page into left/right column bands (top+bottom) plus footer bands, `resize` ~1.7–2.2×, and Read each crop. Reflow column-by-column (whole left column of a page, then whole right). Footnote numbering **restarts every printed page** — keep a per-page→chunk crosswalk and document it in `## Notes`. Page-footer split convention (notes follow body anchors, not the chunk that physically holds the footer text) still applies — always check the last page's footer vs. the next chunk.

### Workflow per CLAUDE.md (locked-in)

> ⚠ For Vol II this list is **modified by the CLAUDE.md "VOL II OVERRIDE"** (top of the Tier-2 verification workflow section). Specifically step 2: the OCR is the base only for clean prose + footnote-marker spacing; the 450 dpi column-band PDF read is authoritative for the (near-universal) cascade-shattered Respondeo/Solutio/footers. Read the override before starting d.2.

1. Find OCR line range; verify boundaries against raw.
2. Latin verbatim from raw OCR, NOT from PDF — *Vol II: except cascade-damaged regions, where the PDF is authoritative (see override above).*
3. `[^N]` anchors at OCR positions, not end-of-clause.
4. English literal, paragraph-for-paragraph.
5. Apparatus walked page-by-page from raw OCR footers.
6. Log `[?]` flags inline; resolve at decade polish-blocker (d.10).
7. Pre-commit: `node site/scripts/build-content.mjs` + the 3 audits `--volume 2` + marker-pairing one-liner.

### Lessons confirmed by sessions 2–4 (carry forward)

- **Trim marginal glosses aggressively** (`Ad oppositum`, `Fundamenta`, `Conclusio`, `Solutio`, `Distinctio`, `Notandum`, `Triplex productio`, `Determinatio trium productionum`, `Epilogus`, `Dupliciter relatio`, `Aliter`, `Eliditur error 1/2/3`, etc.). Don't render them as headings or text.
- **Apparatus marker renumbering is fine** — chunk-internal sequence 1–N is more readable than Quaracchi's per-page restart. Document the page-by-page crosswalk in `## Notes`.
- **Page-break markers** go in the chunk at the printed-page running-head or page-number boundary; if OCR ate a running head, mark `<!-- page N -->` position as `[?]` rather than guessing.
- **Don't try to resolve subtle OCR ambiguities eyes-on-OCR alone** — the d.10 polish pass + 600dpi PDF clears them in seconds.
- **Watch for column-linearization page-spillover** — session 4 caught a "missing" page-30 footer at the top of the next chunk's OCR window. Always glance at the next chunk's first ~20 lines if a footer reference seems short.
- **Cross-chunk footer split is now a confirmed recurring pattern (session 5, page 38).** When a printed page's footer block sits at a chunk boundary, its notes split by *body anchor*, not by which chunk physically contains the footer text. Session 5: page-38 footer notes 1–6 anchored in `d1-p1-dubia`'s page-38 body (DUB III tail/IV/V) but that chunk had only captured pp. 36–37 footer; notes 7–8 anchored in `d1-p2-divisio`. **Before promoting any chunk, verify the prior chunk's last printed page footer was fully captured for the body it holds on that page.** Same convention as `d1-p1-divisio` (notes 1–4 in next chunk's footer). Backfilling a committed Tier-2 chunk is acceptable and expected when this is found — renumber its sequence continuously and document in its Notes + `transcription_status`.
- **Vol II rechunk = `split("\n")`, never `splitlines()` (session 13).** 1056 form feeds in the Vol II raw shift `splitlines()` indices ~1055 off from grep/awk. Symptom: chunks start mid-previous-distinction. Always eyes-on PDF-verify the first chunk's opening line against its intended semantic header after any rechunk.
- **`extract-pages.py --pages` / `colcrop.py` take the PRINTED page number** (the +22 offset is applied internally; output is `p-{printed}.png` / `vol2-p{printed}-…`). Don't pre-convert to PDF page — session 13 wasted an extract doing that.
- **Vol II audit scripts DONE (2026-05-15, commit `76da3e7`)**: all three accept `--volume 2`. Run before each commit alongside the smoke build: `python3.11 tools/audit-{paraphrase,headers,apparatus-count}.py --volume 2 --min-d N --max-d N`. Calibration (verified on d.1's 17 Tier-2 chunks): paraphrase cleanly separates done (→OK) from skeleton (→HIGH/CRITICAL); apparatus-count flag logic intact (0 false flags on real chunks; diff column noisy ±10–18 — triage only); header audit is **coarse for Vol II** (positive diffs, catches only gross whole-chunk dropouts — fine-grained dropout detection still rests on the per-session column-band PDF pass). See CLAUDE.md "Required guard-rail audits" → Vol II calibration block. (`build-content.mjs` is still the parse/marker-pairing check.)

### Pace

- Session 4: 5 short-to-medium chunks in one extended session when apparatus is OCR-clean.
- Session 5: 1 divisio chunk + a cross-chunk apparatus backfill (page-38 footer found mis-split). Cross-chunk corrections cost ~1 chunk-equivalent of effort — budget for them when a chunk boundary falls inside a printed page.
- Session 6: `d1-p2-a1-q1` solo focused session — dense ~1190-word quaestio (pp. 39–40), full 450 dpi PDF pass.
- Session 7: `d1-p2-a1-q2` solo focused session — **largest chunk in d.1** (~2160 words, pp. 41–43, 23 apparatus entries), entire chunk re-set against PDF. A ~2000-word quaestio with heavy cascade damage is a full session on its own; do not bundle.
- Session 8: `d1-p2-a2-q1` solo focused session — ~1300-word quaestio (pp. 43–45, 13 apparatus entries) + re-chunk decision (absorb ART. II opener) + two cross-chunk footer splits (pp. 43, 45) verified against a1-q2's committed crosswalk. Re-chunk + dual cross-chunk split adds ~½ chunk-equivalent over a clean mid-size quaestio. Added `tools/colcrop.py`.
- Session 9: `d1-p2-a2-q2` solo focused session — ~870-word quaestio (pp. 45–47, 13 apparatus entries), full PDF re-set, two cross-chunk footer splits (p.45 note 1 → a2-q1; p.47 notes 3–7 → a3-q1) + a page-45→46 footnote runover folded into [^4]. Mid-size quaestio with two clean cross-chunk splits ≈ one focused session. Two q2-style chunks (a2-q1 + a2-q2) fit in a single working stretch when each is ~900–1300 words and cascade damage is footer-localized — but the dual cross-chunk bookkeeping is the real cost, not word count.
- Session 10: `d1-p2-a3-q1` solo focused session — ~1060-word quaestio (pp. 47–49, 12 apparatus entries) with a *triplex-opinio* Respondeo + three-part scholion, fully two-column-shattered; full 450 dpi PDF re-set. The p.47 cross-chunk footer split (notes 1–2 → a2-q2, 3–7 → a3-q1) was the prior session's open [?] — reading the footer in source order both populated a3-q1 and **retired a2-q2's [^12] flag** in the same pass. Resolving a downstream chunk's flag while building the next chunk is efficient — read shared footers in printed order once and assign both directions.
- Session 11: `d1-p2-a3-q2` solo focused session — ~1330-word quaestio (pp. 49–51, **20 apparatus entries** — the largest apparatus count in d.1 pars 2) with a *triplex-modus* Respondeo, fully two-column-shattered; full 450 dpi PDF re-set. No scholion (a3-q1's covers it — saved a translation pass; always check the prior chunk's scholion for *«pro quaest. seq.»* before assuming a missing scholion is an error). Three sequential cascade sessions (a2-q1→a2-q2→a3-q1→a3-q2) confirm the steady-state cadence: one heavily-damaged ~1000–1300-word quaestio per focused session, ~12–20 apparatus entries, the cross-chunk footer bookkeeping (not word count) being the dominant cost.

- Session 34: **decade-batch re-chunk trial — d.5–d.10 (68 skeletons) in one session** (`93e23bb`). Six distinctions' re-chunk — including two multi-pars (d.7 2525 lines, d.8 1863 lines) and an ARTICULUS-UNICUS-with-9-q (d.9) — done in a single session that would otherwise be ~6 separate full re-chunk sessions under the old interleaved cadence. Method: (1) one DISTINCTIO-level boundary scan disambiguating all 6 semantic-vs-running-head-bleed boundaries; (2) per-distinction internal structure scans (single-pars d.5/d.6/d.9/d.10 mapped in the main thread; the two heavily-garbled multi-pars d.7/d.8 delegated to **two parallel bounded subagents** that returned full chunk lists, then **all 18 consequential boundaries spot-verified in the main thread** — every garbled-header resolution held); (3) wrote the reusable config-driven `tools/rechunk_decade.py`; (4) backup + run (driver's contiguity self-check passed all 6); (5) all 68 first-`## Latin`-lines eyes-on verified + build + 3 audits. **Cost profile vs the d.4 solo re-chunk (s33, one distinction = a full session):** the decade of 6 was ~one session because the structure-mapping parallelized (subagents for the hard multi-pars pair) and the driver amortized — the per-distinction marginal cost collapsed once the driver existed. **Lesson: the re-chunk's expensive part is structure DISCOVERY (garbled-header resolution, pars resolution, boundary disambiguation), not the mechanical regeneration; batching discovery across a decade + a config-driven driver + parallel subagents for the garbled multi-pars distinctions is dramatically more efficient than one-distinction-per-session.** This validates the decade-batch workflow; future decades (d.11–d.20 …) reuse `rechunk_decade.py` with a new `DECADE` config at each polish-gate.
- Session 34 (cont.): **DISTINCTION 4 COMPLETE — all 9 chunks decade-rechunked-skeleton → Tier-2, one session** (`ff9d119`). The full POC of the decade pipeline: littera (`c7dc22f`) + divisio (`f3632a9`) + 6 cascade quaestiones (`5667b0f`/`cad5180`/`b90290d`/`17abc37`/`47a2f32`/`1065ea0`) + dubia (`ff9d119`), per-chunk build+3-audits+marker-pairing+commit. Final d.4 = audit-paraphrase critical 0 / HIGH 0; headers no LOSS; 9/9 `Phase C Tier 2 complete`; build 471 tr. **Cost profile:** littera+divisio ≈ ½ session each (divisio's page already read for the littera); each cascade quaestio ≈ the d.1–d.3 envelope; the two largest (a1-q2 ~2850 w pp.132–135 w/ Scholion I–III; a3-q1 ~2370 w pp.138–141 4-page cascade) were the dominant cost; dubia light. **Lesson reaffirmed (4×+ this session): cross-chunk footer splits must be resolved by *content/lemma/column*, not position** — the trickiest was a3-q2↔dubia where p.142's footer splits BY COLUMN (left-col 1–5 a3-q2's Solutio, right-col 6–8 dubia's DUB I/II), confirmed only on the L-2/R-2 crops; also a2-q2→a3-q1 where the p.138 footer is linearized inside a3-q1's raw range (n.6 = a3-q1's *quasi per totum*, n.1–5 a2-q2's). **Lesson: sibling-scholion coverage is real and load-bearing in d.4** — a2-q2 and a3-q2 have NO own scholion (a2-q1/a3-q1 scholia say "hanc et seq. quaestionem" / are cross-ref'd by the next q's last footer note `Vide Schol. praecedentis/praeced. quaest.`); always read that terminal cross-ref note before flagging a missing scholion. **POC PROVEN: decade-batch rechunk → main-thread Tier-2 works for every Vol II chunk type.** Cadence going forward (d.5→d.10, uninterrupted): ~1 cascade quaestio per focused stretch, littera/divisio faster, the per-chunk commit + the per-page colcrop L-then-R discipline unchanged.

## Tools cheat sheet

```bash
# Inspect raw chunk range
awk 'NR>={start} && NR<={end}' raw/bonaventure_vol2_raw.txt

# Vol II offset: pdf_page = printed + 22
python3.11 tools/extract-pages.py --volume vol2 --pages {printed_pages}

# Chunker warnings
python3.11 tools/auto-chunk-volume.py 2 --dry-run 2>&1 | awk '/WARNINGS/,/Chunk list/'

# Smoke build
cd site && node scripts/build-content.mjs
```

Vol II offsets: see CLAUDE.md table (`pdf = printed + 22`). No deploy until meaningful Vol II Tier-2 work to show.
